# Restaurant365

> Knowledge base documentation for Restaurant365.

## doc

- [R365 Knowledge Base](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/r365-knowledge-base.md): Browse R365 documentation for accounting, operations, labor, purchasing, and platform setup. Start here to find the article you need.
- [Create a R365 Support Center Login](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/support-center-user-account.md): Register a Support Center account to create tickets, check status, and reopen closed tickets in Freshdesk—separate from your R365 login.
- [R365 Support](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/r365-support.md): Contact R365 support, submit tickets, and access self-service resources including the knowledge base and community forums.
- [User Research](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/user-research.md): Participate in R365 R&amp;D through usability studies, observation calls, onsite visits, and beta testing. Sign up via the interest form.
- [R365 Branded Emails](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/r365-branded-emails.md): R365 branded email templates apply to password resets, custom financial reports, report subscriptions, and workflows.
- [Help Menu](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/help-menu.md)
- [Help Menu](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/help-menus.md): Access page-specific video guides, KB widget, R365 University, Support Center, webinars, release notes, and idea submission from the Help menu.
- [Video Guides](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/r365-academy-videos.md): Launch in-app video guides from the Help menu. Play inline, use mini-player while working, or switch to theater mode.
- [Knowledge Base Widget](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/knowledge-base-widget.md): In-app widget surfaces contextual KB articles. Supports AI search, drag to reposition, full-screen expand, and new-tab viewing.
- [Submit an Idea](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/submit-an-idea.md): Submit product feedback via the Customer Idea Portal from the Help menu. Fill in role, product line, and summary, then review and submit.
- [Find Your R365 Instance](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/r365-instance.md): Find your R365 instance URL in the browser address bar or login page. Learn what to do if you don't know it and how to bookmark it.
- [Logging In](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/logging-in.md)
- [Log In to R365 (Desktop)](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/log-in-to-r365.md): Log in to R365 on a web browser using your instance URL, username, and password or SSO. Includes logout steps and troubleshooting link.
- [Log In to the Legacy R365 App](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/logging-in-to-the-r365-app-1.md)
- [Troubleshoot User Login Issues](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/troubleshoot-user-login-issues-1.md): Fix common R365 login failures: wrong credentials, incorrect database URL, missing user roles, location access issues, and browser cache problems.
- [R365 Navigation](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/r365-navigation-category.md)
- [R365 Navigation](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/r365-navigation.md): Navigate R365 using the left navigation rail and module menus. Covers Admin, Operations, and primary navigation areas.
- [R365 Search](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/r365-search.md): Use the global search bar in R365 to find records, pages, and articles. Learn search syntax, filter results, and navigate to matches.
- [Multi-Instance Switching](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/multi-instance-switching-category.md)
- [Multi-Instance Switching Overview](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/multi-instance-switching.md): Switch between connected brand or partner instances with a single sign-in. Use the header switcher, Instances screen, nicknames, and favorites.
- [Instances Screen](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/my-instances.md): View all accessible R365 instances, search, filter by All or Favorites, set nicknames, and star favorites from the Instances screen.
- [Multi-Instance Users](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/multi-instance-login.md): Multi-instance switching requires matching usernames and verified emails for brand instances, or matching emails for partner-managed instances.
- [Instance Nicknames for Multi-Instance Switching](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/multi-instance-nicknames.md): Set a custom display name for an instance in the multi-instance switcher. Requires Edit Instance Nicknames permission; changes apply to all users.
- [Set Favorite Instances for Multi-Instance Switching](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/set-favorite-instances-for-multi-instance-switching.md): Mark or unmark R365 instances as favorites on the Instances screen so they appear at the top of the multi-instance switcher dropdown.
- [Notifications](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/notifications-folder.md)
- [Notifications Overview](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/notifications-overview.md): R365 Notifications alert users to approvals, exceptions, and system events. Learn notification types and where to view them in-app.
- [Notification Center](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/notifications-notification-center.md): Enable or disable notifications and choose push, email, or SMS delivery by module in the Notification Center settings screen.
- [Notification Window](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/notification-window.md): Open the Notification panel via the Bell icon to view unread alerts, mark all as read, or toggle read/unread per notification.
- [Manage Notifications](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/manage-notifications.md): Configure R365 notification preferences: enable or disable specific types and set delivery options per user.
- [Using R365](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/using-r365.md)
- [Keyboard Shortcuts](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/keyboard-shortcuts.md): R365 keyboard shortcuts for saving records, navigating fields, and opening common pages faster.
- [Working With Smart Grids](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/working-with-smart-grids.md): Use Smart Grids to filter, sort, manage columns, run bulk actions, export, and save custom grid views for reuse.
- [Working with Classic List Views](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/working-with-list-views.md): Sort, filter, customize columns, export data, and navigate records in Classic List Views within R365's legacy interface.
- [Filter a Table](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/filtering-tables.md): Apply filters to R365 tables via the filter panel. Set conditions, combine multiple filters, and clear filters as needed.
- [Adjust Table Columns](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/adjust-table-columns.md): Show, hide, reorder, and resize columns in R365 tables using column management options in the table menu.
- [Filter a Table With Column Filters](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/filter-a-table-with-column-filters.md): Filter R365 table rows directly from column headers without the main filter panel. Narrow results by specific field values.
- [Change Table Row Height](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/change-table-row-height.md): Adjust table row density to Tall, Medium, or Short in R365's new table layout via the Menu button. Settings are per-session only.
- [Manage Table Views](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/manage-table-views.md): Create, save, rename, and switch between custom table views in R365 to preserve column, filter, and sort configurations.
- [Export Grid Data](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/export-grid-data.md): Export visible filtered R365 grid rows to CSV from standard or classic grid layouts. No special permissions required.
- [Apply Striped Rows To Tables](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/apply-row-stripping-to-tables.md): Enable or disable alternating gray row shading on R365 tables via the table menu. Not all tables support striped rows.
- [Address Verification](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/address-verification.md): R365 validates and standardizes vendor or location addresses against USPS data to ensure accuracy for payments and records.
- [Version History Panel](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/version-panel.md): View past, current, and future saved versions of a record in the Version History panel. Browse version cards and effective dates.
- [Activity Log](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/activity-feed.md): View a timestamped action history per record. Filter by All, Activity, or Comments; search entries; @mention users; expand action details.
- [Attachments Tab](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/attachments-tab.md): Manage all files on an R365 record from the Attachments tab. Upload, view, download, or delete attachments in one place.
- [Attachment Viewer](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/attachment-viewer.md): View AP transaction attachments side-by-side in the Attachment Viewer sidebar. Navigate, zoom, rotate, and download files.
- [Upload File](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/upload-file.md): Attach files to any R365 record using the Upload File button—useful for AP invoices. Delete uploaded files with the X icon.
- [How to Import Excel CSV with Leading Zeros](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/how-to-import-excel-csv-with-leading-zeros.md): Prevent Excel from stripping leading zeros in CSV imports by formatting columns as Text before saving. Applies to AP invoice and R365 imports.
- [Using Restaurant365 in Other Languages](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/using-restaurant365-in-other-languages.md): R365 web is English-only. The mobile app supports English and Spanish. Use Chrome's translate feature for other languages.
- [R365 Mobile Language Options](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/r365-mobile-language-options.md): Change the R365 mobile app language to English, Spanish, or Canadian French in User Profile. UI elements only; user data is not translated.
- [System Requirements](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/system-requirements.md)
- [Restaurant365 System Requirements](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/restaurant365-system-requirements.md): Hardware specs for Windows and Mac, broadband requirements, Chrome browser, POS server specs, and spreadsheet access requirements.
- [R365 Supported Browser](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/r365-supported-browser.md): Google Chrome is the only officially supported browser for R365. Other browsers may work but are not fully tested.
- [R365 Domains](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/r365-domain.md): An R365 domain is the unique subdomain before .restaurant365.com. Select it when logging into the mobile app with a non-unique User ID.
- [Clear Cache and Cookies on Web Browser](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/clear-cache-and-cookies-on-web-browser.md): Clear cache and cookies in Chrome, Firefox, or Safari to resolve R365 display or performance issues. Links to official browser guides.
- [R365 Security Controls](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/r365-security-controls.md): Download the R365 security controls white sheet summarizing data protection practices and security controls.
- [R365 Website Shortcut on Tablet](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/r365-website-shortcut-on-tablet.md): Add an R365 web page shortcut to your Home screen on iPhone, iPad (Safari), or Android (Chrome) for quick daily access.
- [Scheduled Maintenance](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/scheduled-maintenance.md): R365 performs regular maintenance for upgrades and new features. In-app notifications announce maintenance windows.
- [R365 Public API](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/r365-public-api.md): Access R365's Public API for seamless data integration, supporting reads/writes across key areas like Accounting, Inventory, and Sales.
- [Getting Started](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/getting-started.md)
- [R365 Implementation Process (Accounting and Operations)](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/what-does-the-onboarding-process-look-like.md): R365 onboarding overview for Accounting (integrations, AP, payroll, cutover) and Operations (items, inventory, recipes) with milestone checklists.
- [Getting Started with Accounting](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/getting-started-with-accounting.md)
- [Golden Rules of Accounting](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/golden-rules-of-accounting.md): R365 accounting best practices: review DSS before approval, set Payroll JE dates, match bank activity regularly, and close periods on time.
- [Phase 1: Getting Started & Discovery](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/phase-1-getting-started-discovery.md): Phase 1: set up Legal Entities, Locations, and Fiscal Calendar; import Chart of Accounts; kick off POS and vendor integrations.
- [Phase 2: Money In](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/phase-2-money-in.md): Phase 2: configure POS, approve Daily Sales, record bank deposits, set up third-party delivery, and run P&amp;L reports. Timeline: 2–3 weeks.
- [Phase 3: Money Out](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/phase-3-money-out.md): Phase 3: set up vendors, AP invoicing, manual and run-based payments, and bank expense recording. Covers AP Capture and R365 Payments.
- [Phase 4: Accounting Cutover](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/phase-4-accounting-cutover.md): Phase 4 checklist: finalize legacy period-end, match starting balances, import open AP/AR and uncleared bank transactions in R365.
- [Accounting Cutover Process](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/accounting-cutover-process.md): Go Live cutover: match legacy balances to R365 starting balances, migrate open AP/AR and bank transactions. Includes cutover FAQs.
- [Phase 5: Banking](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/phase-5-banking.md): Phase 5: learn undeposited funds, bank expenses vs. AP payments, and complete a Bank Reconciliation against the Balance Sheet.
- [Phase 6: Payroll](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/phase-6-payroll.md): Covers recording payroll in R365: creating payroll JEs, reversing accruals, and reconciling banking transactions.
- [Accounting Onboarding to Adoption](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/accounting-onboarding-to-adoption.md): Final onboarding phase: self-audit your database, use the Support Center, complete DWM tasks, and transition to ongoing R365 adoption.
- [Key Accounting Reports](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/key-accounting-reports.md): Overview of six core R365 accounting reports: P&amp;L, GL Account Detail, Balance Sheet, AP Aging, AR Aging, and Bank Reconciliation.
- [Using Monday.com](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/using-mondaycom.md): Use Monday.com during R365 onboarding to track implementation tasks, access call recordings, and submit files to your R365 Coach.
- [Exporting Data from QuickBooks®](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/exporting-data-from-quickbooks.md)
- [Export Chart of Accounts List From QuickBooks® Desktop](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/export-chart-of-accounts-list-from-quickbooks-desktop.md): Export the Account Listing from QuickBooks Desktop to CSV, number accounts with R365's suggested structure, and prepare for R365 import.
- [Export Chart of Accounts List From QuickBooks® Online](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/export-chart-of-accounts-list-from-quickbooks-online.md): Export, number, and reformat the Account List from QuickBooks Online for import into R365 using NRA-based account numbering.
- [Export Vendor List From QuickBooks® Desktop](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/export-vendor-list-from-quickbooks-desktop.md): Download, deduplicate, and reformat the Vendor Contact List from QuickBooks Desktop for import into R365 via Setup Assistant.
- [Export Vendor List From QuickBooks® Online](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/export-vendor-list-from-quickbooks-online.md): Download, clean, and reformat your QuickBooks Online vendor list as a CSV file for import into R365.
- [Export Customer List From QuickBooks® Desktop](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/export-customer-list-from-quickbooks-desktop.md): Export the Customer Contact List from QuickBooks Desktop as CSV, remove duplicates, and reformat it for import into R365.
- [Export Customer List From QuickBooks® Online](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/export-customer-list-from-quickbooks-online.md): Export the Customer Contact List from QuickBooks Online, remove duplicates, and reformat as CSV for import into R365.
- [Pulling Data from QuickBooks® Desktop for R365 Migration](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/pulling-data-from-quickbooks-desktop-for-r365-migration.md): Export Trial Balance and Transaction Detail reports from QuickBooks Desktop on accrual basis for R365 migration. Send to the Data Import team.
- [Pulling Data from QuickBooks® Online for R365 Migration](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/pulling-data-from-quickbooks-online-for-r365-migration.md): Export two Trial Balances and two Transaction Detail reports from QuickBooks Online for R365 migration. Send to a Data Import Specialist.
- [Completing a Crosswalk](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/completing-a-crosswalk.md): Complete the account mapping Crosswalk file during R365 migration: review field definitions, enter mappings, and handle duplicates.
- [Getting Started with Operations](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/getting-started-with-operations.md)
- [Golden Rules of Operations](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/golden-rules-of-operations.md): Best-practice dos and don'ts for R365 Operations covering UofMs, Purchased Items, Vendor Items, Inventory Counts, Recipes, and Menu Items.
- [Using Monday.com](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/using-mondaycom-1.md)
- [Preparation for Operations](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/preparation-for-operations.md): Pre-onboarding guide for Operations Power Users: qualifications, call expectations, topics to prepare, and documents to gather.
- [Phase 1: Items In](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/phase-1-items-in.md): Ops Phase 1: import Purchased Items, Units of Measure, and Vendor Items; map Item Categories to GL Accounts. Timeline: 3–4 weeks.
- [Phase 2: Item Management and Transactions](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/phase-2-item-management-and-transactions.md): Ops Phase 2: set up itemized invoicing, map vendor items, configure manager users, and establish daily AP tasks. Timeline: 3–4 weeks.
- [Phase 3: Inventory](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/phase-3-inventory.md): Ops Phase 3: build inventory count templates, train managers, and align beginning balance with accounting calendar. Timeline: 2–3 weeks.
- [Phase 4: Recipes](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/phase-4-recipes.md): Ops Phase 4: learn recipe and menu item concepts, map recipes to POS items for COGS tracking and theoretical depletion. Timeline: 2 weeks.
- [Phase 5: Scheduling](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/phase-5-scheduling.md): Ops Phase 5: set up Scheduling, update employee records, exclude jobs, and train managers on shift creation. Timeline: 1–2 weeks.
- [Phase 6: Ops Onboarding to Adoption](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/phase-6-ops-onboarding-to-adoption.md): Final ops onboarding phase: self-audit your database, use the Support Center, and build daily/weekly/monthly R365 habits. Timeline: 1 week.
- [Operations Reporting Essentials](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/reporting-essentials.md): Video guide to key ops reports: Purchasing, Actual vs. Theoretical, P&amp;L Comparison, Weekly Review, and Profit &amp; Loss Layout.
- [Restaurant Operational Reports List](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/restaurant-operational-reports-list.md): R365 operational reports index: Inventory, Sales, Labor, Vendor, Customer, Human Resources, and Location reports with links.
- [Key Operations Reports](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/key-operations-reports.md): Key ops reports with frequency chart: Actual vs. Theoretical, Weekly Review, P&amp;L Comparison, Vital Signs, COGS Analysis, and purchasing.
- [Key Labor Reports](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/key-labor-reports.md): Five essential labor reports: Flash Report, Weekly Review–Location, Overtime Warning, Sales and Labor Breakout, and Labor Actual vs. Scheduled.
- [Building Great Habits](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/building-great-habits.md)
- [Daily, Weekly, Monthly, and Quarterly Tasks (Accounting and Operations)](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/daily-weekly-monthly-quarterly-tasks-accounting-operations.md): Master DWM checklist linking all recurring R365 accounting and operations tasks by daily, weekly, monthly, and quarterly frequency.
- [Quarterly Accounting Self-Audit](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/quarterly-accounting-self-audit.md): Quarterly accounting checklist: clear AP/AR aging, review user permissions, reconcile bank deposits, and review third-party transactions.
- [Quarterly Operations Self-Audit](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/quarterly-operations-self-audit.md): Quarterly ops checklist: validate item setup and UofMs with the Wellness Check Workbook and review labor setup, employee status, and break rules.
- [Adoption Journey](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/adoption-journey-folder.md)
- [Adoption Journey Roadmap](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/adoption-journey.md): Post-implementation Adoption Journey: 4-quarter roadmap using the DWM Checklist to build healthy R365 habits with support resources.
- [Setup Review and Daily Tasks](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/setup-review-and-daily-tasks.md): Maintain daily R365 tasks via To Do Checklist and DSS review. Consistent daily habits support faster period close and accurate reporting.
- [Continuing Education Webinar Series](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/continuing-education-webinar-series.md): Recorded R365 webinars covering CFR Writer, Budgeting, Reporting 101, Bank Reconciliation, Item Maintenance, Recipes, and Top 5 Reports.
- [Weekly Tasks and Reports](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/weekly-tasks-and-reports.md): Weekly R365 habits: verify payroll accrual reversals, check item costs, review and approve inventory counts, and analyze labor variances.
- [Inventory Clean Up Process](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/inventory-clean-up-process.md): 8-step inventory cleanup: categorize items, rename, flag duplicates, review UofMs, remap Vendor Items, verify equivalences, rebuild count templates.
- [Monthly Tasks and Reports](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/monthly-tasks-and-reports.md): Monthly tasks: bank reconciliation, inventory count, and review Balance Sheet, P&amp;L, Actual vs. Theoretical, and Menu Item Analysis reports.
- [Quarterly Tasks and Reports](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/quarterly-tasks-and-reports.md): Two quarterly self-audits: Accounting (AP/AR aging, user permissions) and Operations (item setup, GL mapping, labor). Set a recurring reminder.
- [Quarterly Recap](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/quarterly-recap.md): End-of-quarter recap: DWM task benefits including timely period close, accurate COGS, vendor accountability, and location profitability.
- [Defining Your Data](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/defining-your-data.md): Use R365 metrics, labor goals, sales forecasts, and key labor reports to build a data-driven culture and manage restaurant costs.
- [Visualizing Your Data with Dashboards](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/visualizing-your-data-with-dashboards.md): Three R365 dashboards: Labor (YoY labor cost), Operations (hourly sales/labor), and Financial (COGS%, EBIT, profit margins).
- [Restaurant365 Certifications Overview](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/restaurant365-certifications-overview.md): R365 certifications for Accounting and Operations Foundations: eligibility, 85% passing score, exam format, topics, and digital credentials.
- [Release Notes](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/release-notes.md)
- [Release Notes: New Releases](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/release-notes-new-releases.md): Published R365 release notes covering new features, enhancements, and bug fixes across all modules. Updated with each product release.

- [Legal Entity Setup in the Onboarding Dashboard](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/legal-entity-setup.md): Set up your Legal Entity during R365 onboarding: enter legal name, address, and EIN. Add multiple entities before creating locations.
- [Location Setup in the Onboarding Dashboard](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/location-step.md): Create locations during R365 onboarding with name, address, legal entity, and timezone. Required before calendar and POS setup.
- [Calendar Setup in the Onboarding Dashboard](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/calendar-setup.md): Configure fiscal and operational calendars during R365 onboarding: set period dates, work week definition, and assign to locations.
- [Start of Business Day Setup in the Onboarding Dashboard](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/start-of-business-day-setup.md): Configure Start of Business Day during onboarding to set when each location's business day begins. Must match your POS reset time.
- [POS Setup in the Onboarding Dashboard](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/pos-setup.md): Request POS integration per location in the Onboarding Dashboard. Submitting creates an R365 support ticket to coordinate integration setup.
- [Vendor Integrations Setup in the Onboarding Dashboard](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/vendor-integrations-setup.md): Connect supported vendors to R365 for automatic AP invoice imports during onboarding. Enter vendor details, OPCO codes, and assign locations.
- [Setup Legal Entities in the Onboarding Dashboard](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/onboarding-dashboard-create-a-legal-entity.md): Create legal entities during R365 onboarding: enter legal name, address, and EIN. Required before locations can be created.
- [Setup Locations in the Onboarding Dashboard](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/onboarding-dashboard-create-a-location.md): Create R365 locations during onboarding: enter name, address, legal entity, and timezone to unlock subsequent setup steps.
- [Setup Calendars in the Onboarding Dashboard](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/onboarding-dashboard-create-a-calendar.md): Create fiscal and operational calendars during R365 onboarding: enter calendar name, period dates, work week start day, and assign to locations.
- [Setup Start of Business Day in the Onboarding Dashboard](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/onboarding-dashboard-create-a-start-of-business-day.md): Set Start of Business Day during R365 onboarding: name groups, assign locations, and set effective date and start time to match POS.
- [Setup POS Integrations in the Onboarding Dashboard](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/setup-pos-integration-in-the-onboarding-dashboard.md): Submit POS integration requests during R365 onboarding: select POS system, enter connection details per location, and create a support ticket.
- [Setup Vendor Integrations in the Onboarding Dashboard](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/setup-vendor-integrations-in-the-onboarding-dashboard.md): Submit vendor integration requests during R365 onboarding: select vendor, enter contact and OPCO details, assign locations, and submit.

- [R365 Custom Financial Reports Writer: Building Reports](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/r365-custom-financial-reports-writer-building-reports.md): Build CFR reports by creating templates with row and column layouts, adding reports, setting org unit filters, and using NRA example reports.

- [R365 Custom Financial Reports Writer: Report Columns](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/r365-custom-financial-reports-writer-report-columns.md): Configure CFR template column layouts: add data, calculation, and blank columns; set date periods, org unit settings, and formatting.
- [R365 Custom Financial Reports Writer: Creating Reports](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/r365-custom-financial-reports-writer-creating-reports.md): Create a Custom Financial Report from a template. Set org unit filters, viewer access, and date parameters, then save or run from the list.
- [R365 Custom Financial Reports Writer: Combination Reports](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/r365-custom-financial-reports-writer-combination-reports.md): Create CFR combination reports that merge P&amp;L and Balance Sheet types into a single output with shared column and row layouts.
- [R365 Custom Financial Report Writer: Running Reports](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/r365-custom-financial-report-writer-running-reports.md): Run Custom Financial Reports: select org units, date parameters, and filters; export to PDF or Excel or schedule automated delivery.
- [R365 Custom Financial Reports: Assigning Reports](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/r365-custom-financial-reports-assigning-reports.md): Grant users access to Custom Financial Reports via the Financial Report Viewer field or Report Role Setup page.
- [R365 Custom Financial Reports Writer: Report Packages](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/r365-custom-financial-reports-writer-report-packages.md): Create and run CFR Report Packages that bundle multiple reports. Configure repeat settings, org units, and GL Detail inclusion per item.
- [R365 Custom Financial Report Writer: Report Groups](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/r365-custom-financial-report-writer-report-groups.md): Create, run, and edit Report Groups within CFR Report Packages. Control report ordering by org unit or repeat value.
- [R365 Custom Financial Report Writer: Reports with Multiple Fiscal Year Structures](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/r365-custom-financial-report-writer-reports-with-multiple-fiscal-year-structures.md): CFR uses one Fiscal Structure per report run. Run entities with different fiscal structures in separate runs or Report Packages.
- [R365 Custom Financial Reports Writer: Report Viewer](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/r365-custom-financial-reports-writer-report-viewer.md): Report Viewer: view, navigate, and export completed Custom Financial Reports with drill-down, column sorting, and export options.

- [Duplicate a CFR report](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/duplicate-a-report-in-cfr.md): Duplicate a CFR report by creating a new template and assigning the same Row and Column Layouts from the original.

- [AI Dashboards: Sales Dashboard Master](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/r365-intelligence-sales-dashboard-master.md): Sales Dashboard Master shows gross and net sales trends, location comparisons, check count, guest count, and performance across time periods.
- [AI Dashboards: PMIX Dashboard Master](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/r365-intelligence-pmix-dashboard-master.md): PMIX Dashboard Master shows menu item sales counts, mix percentages, and performance broken down by item and category.

- [AI Dashboards: Intraday Dashboard Master](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/r365-intelligence-intraday-dashboard-master.md): Intraday Dashboard shows real-time sales data throughout the day. Requires intraday polling to be active on your account.
- [AI Dashboards: Menu Engineering Dashboard Master](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/r365-intelligence-menu-engineering-dashboard-master.md): Menu Engineering Dashboard: analyze menu item profitability with Bubble Chart, Heat Map, Top 10/Bottom 10, and what-if scenario support.
- [AI Dashboards: Key Info Comparative Dashboard Master](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/r365-intelligence-key-info-comparative-dashboard-master.md): Key Info Comparative Dashboard shows DSS data vs. Prior Year, Prior Week, OWTD, OPTD, and OYTD across four pages.

- [Creating Attributes in AI Dashboards](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/r365-intelligence-creating-attributes.md): Create custom derived attributes in AI Dashboards using the Attribute Editor or by changing an existing attribute's data type.
- [Creating Metrics in AI Dashboards](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/r365-intelligence-creating-metrics.md): Create custom AI Dashboard metrics using shortcut metrics, Aggregate By functions, or the Formula/Function editor. Set behavior to Manual.
- [Using Filters in AI Dashboards](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/r365-intelligence-using-filters.md): Add chapter-level filters, visualization filters, and interactive selectors (element, value, attribute/metric, panel, parameters) to AI Dashboards.
- [Creating Element Groups in AI Dashboards](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/r365-intelligence-creating-element-groups.md): Combine attribute elements into custom groups in AI Dashboard visualizations. Create element groupings or calculated groups from the editor panel.
- [Adding Thresholds to Visualizations in AI Dashboards](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/r365-intelligence-adding-thresholds-to-visualizations.md): Add thresholds to AI Dashboard metrics: set type, color, Break By, margin selector, and use the Advanced Threshold Editor for fine-tuned ranges.
- [Formatting a Visualization in AI Dashboards](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/r365-intelligence-formatting-a-visualization.md): Format AI Dashboard visualizations using Visualization Options (chart shape, axes), Text and Form (font, color), and Title and Container subtabs.
- [AI Dashboards: Troubleshoot Join Errors](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/troubleshoot-join-errors.md): Resolve cartesian join errors in AI Dashboards by finding mismatched objects in the editor panel and removing the conflicting attribute or metric.

Save &amp; Close or Save &amp; Activate.

- [Mark a Preset Allocation Active or Inactive](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/preset-allocations-mark-as-inactive.md): Set a Preset Allocation Active or Inactive in bulk via Edit Selected on the list page, or individually from the allocation sidesheet Status dropdown.
- [Journal Entries](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/journal-entries.md)
- [Journal Entry Overview](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/journal-entry-overview.md): Overview of R365 journal entries: manual creation, import, system-generated entries, available actions, permissions, and allocation integrations.
- [Journal Entry Record](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/journal-entry-record.md): Field reference for the R365 journal entry record: General Information tab, Detail lines, Statistical tab, Attachments tab, and Activity Feed.
- [Create a Journal Entry](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/new-journal-entry.md): Manually create a journal entry in R365: enter header fields, add GL account detail lines with debits/credits, then save or approve the entry.
- [Journal Entry Type](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/journal-entry-type.md): R365 journal entry types — Standard, Amortization, Adjustment, Beginning Balance, and Payroll — and where each type appears in list views.
- [Create a Memorized Journal Entry](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/creating-a-memorized-journal-entry.md): Create a memorized journal entry in R365 as a reusable or auto-posting recurring template on a weekly, monthly, or custom schedule.
- [Reverse a Journal Entry](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/reversal-journal-entry.md): Create a reversing journal entry in R365 via the Action menu. Choose Start/End of Next Period, Custom, or None as the reversal date type.
- [Import Journal Entries](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/importing-journal-entries.md): Import standard or payroll journal entries via CSV: select template, fill fields, set import parameters, and troubleshoot common import errors.
- [Journal Entry Import Templates](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/journal-entry-import-templates.md): Download R365 CSV import templates for standard and payroll journal entries. Supported payroll providers: Paychex, ADP Workforce, and Big Fish.
- [Spread a Transaction](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/transaction-spreading.md): Spread a journal entry across multiple future periods at a defined cadence using the transaction spreading feature in R365.
- [Journal Entry Update 2026](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/journal-entry-update-2025.md): 2026 journal entry UI changes: updated action menu, Statistical tab, Attachments tab with split-view preview, Activity Feed, and collapsible header.
- [Payroll Journal Entries](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/payroll-journal-entries.md)
- [Payroll Journal Entry Overview](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/payroll-journal-entry-overview.md): Payroll JEs in R365: how DSS labor accruals are created, how actual payroll JEs replace estimates, liability tracking, and the clearing process.
- [Approve a Payroll Journal Entry](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/approve-a-payroll-journal-entry.md): Approve a payroll JE to finalize labor clearing. Covers prerequisites, Payroll Estimate Clearing tab, Distribution variances, and job title mapping.
- [Payroll Journal Entry Record](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/payroll-journal-entry-record.md): Payroll JE record reference: Details tab (actual labor), Distribution tab (variances), Payroll Estimate Clearing tab, pay period dates, and headers.
- [Create a Payroll Journal Entry](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/create-a-payroll-journal-entry.md): Manually create a payroll journal entry in R365: enter pay period dates, add wages, taxes, and deductions as line items, then save or approve.
- [Import a Payroll Journal Entry](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/import-a-payroll-journal-entry.md): Import payroll data using the Payroll JE Import Template CSV: required fields, payroll start/end dates, and the Import Journal Entry page steps.
- [Estimated and Actual Payroll Journal Entries](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/est-act-payroll-journal-entries.md): DSS labor JEs estimate payroll costs from POS data; actual payroll JEs clear those estimates. Covers Distribution and Payroll Estimate Clearing tabs.
- [Payroll Liability](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/payroll-liability.md): The Accrued Payroll Account tracks daily labor accruals from DSS. Accruals reverse when a payroll JE posts, netting the GL account to zero.
- [Unapprove a DSS after Running Payroll](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/unapprove-a-dss-after-running-payroll.md): Fix uncleared payroll accruals: unapprove the payroll JE, then unapprove and repoll the DSS. Uses Distribution tab and GL Account Detail report.
- [Gross Sales vs Net Sales](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/gross-sales-vs-net-sales.md): R365 non-P&amp;L reports show Net Sales by default. Show gross sales instead by reclassifying Discounts &amp; Comps accounts to a different GL Type.
- [Transactions](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/transactions.md)
- [Approve a Transaction](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/approve-a-transaction.md): Approve R365 transactions across accounting and inventory types: approve menu actions, GL posting, inventory impact, and permissions by type.
- [Unapprove a Transaction](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/unapprove-a-transaction.md): Unapprove an approved transaction in R365: required permissions, step-by-step process, and handling transactions in closed fiscal periods.
- [Searching Transactions](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/searching-transactions.md): Find transactions in R365 using the All Transactions list: filter by amount, date, location, or GL account, then export results to Excel.
- [Intercompany Transactions](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/intercompany-transactions.md): R365 auto-creates Due To/Due From entries for multi-Legal Entity transactions. Two setup methods for intercompany accounts on the Legal Entity record.
- [Determine if a Transaction is in a Closed Fiscal Period](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/deterime-if-a-transaction-is-in-a-closed-fiscal-period.md): Identify if a transaction's GL date falls in a closed fiscal period using All Transactions and Legal Entity records for Advanced or standard close.
- [Unapprove Button Disappeared](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/unapprove-button-disappeared.md): Missing Unapprove button means the transaction is in a closed fiscal period. Reopen the period to restore the Unapprove option.
- [Edit an Approved Transaction](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/edit-an-approved-transaction.md): Edit the detail grid of an approved AP Invoice, AP Credit Memo, AR Transaction, JE, or Inventory Count using Edit and Edit Complete in R365.
- [Delete a Transaction](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/delete-a-transaction.md): Delete a transaction in R365: requirements (open period, no applied payments, unapproved), steps, and how to unapply AP payments or credit memos.
- [Reasons to Unapprove a Transaction](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/reasons-to-unapprove-a-transaction.md): Common reasons to unapprove transactions: AP Invoices (missing mappings), Journal Entries (user error), and DSS (bad data, payroll mismatch).
- [Complete a Transaction](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/complete-a-transaction.md): The Complete step for AP Invoices, Counts, Transfers, and Waste Logs differs from Approve. Learn required permissions by inventory transaction type.

- [Missing and Out of Balance Daily Sales Summaries](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/troubleshooting-missing-and-out-of-balance-daily-sales-summary.md): Troubleshoot missing DSS records and out-of-balance journal entries. Compare POS reports to DSS data for major POS systems.
- [Manually Delete a DSS](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/manually-delete-a-dss.md): Manually delete a Daily Sales Summary record. R365 re-imports deleted DSSs on the next scheduled POS poll automatically.
- [Uploading Deposits via FTP](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/uploading-deposits-via-ftp.md): Import deposits in bulk via FTP using a CSV with location number, date, deposit number, and deposit amount columns.
- [Forecasting](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/forecasting-category.md)
- [Forecasting Overview](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/forecasting.md): Introduction to R365 Forecasting for predicting weekly sales and labor using historical data, projection dates, and events.
- [Forecasting Page](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/weekly-forecasting-forecasting-page.md): Reference for the weekly and daily Forecasting page showing week/day views, actual vs. forecast comparisons, and location navigation.
- [Adjusting Forecasts](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/weekly-forecasting-adjusting-forecasts.md): Manually adjust sales forecasts by modifying projected amounts, adding revenue-impacting events, and overriding algorithm values.
- [Review Projection Dates Page](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/weekly-forecasting-review-projection-dates-page.md): Review and manage sales projection dates for weekly forecasting and adjust projection date settings per location.
- [Customizing Projection Dates](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/weekly-forecasting-customizing-projection-dates.md): Include or exclude specific historical dates from sales projections on the Review Projection Dates page. Changes must be applied.
- [Publishing Forecasts](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/weekly-forecasting-publishing-forecasts.md): Publish or re-publish a weekly forecast to finalize sales and labor projections for scheduling and ordering. Re-publishing overwrites drafts.
- [Labor Forecasting by Job](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/custom-labor-forecasting.md): Adjust labor forecasts by job in day view using the Custom Labor Matrix. Edit hours per job per time interval and republish.
- [Import Sales Forecast](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/import-sales-forecast.md): Import a manually created sales forecast via template by downloading, adding data, uploading, and reviewing failed rows.
- [Import Historical Sales and Entrees for Use in Forecasting](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/import-historical-sales-and-entrees-for-use-in-forecasting.md): Import historical sales and entree data to seed weekly forecasting. Covers permissions, import steps, and correcting failed rows.
- [Forecasting Settings](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/forecasting-settings-category.md)
- [Forecasting Settings](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/weekly-forecasting-settings.md): Overview of Weekly Forecasting Settings including the Configurations tab for metrics and the Rules tab for forecast rule groups.
- [Forecast Configurations Tab](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/forecast-configurations.md): View and manage forecast configuration groups on the Configurations tab. Each group sets metric, projection model, and history weeks.
- [Create and Edit Forecast Configuration Groups](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/create-and-edit-forecast-configuration-groups.md): Edit or create forecast configuration groups by enabling metrics, setting default projection model, and choosing projection weeks.
- [Projection Models and Forecast Metrics](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/weekly-forecasting-projection-models-and-forecast-metrics.md): Reference for weekly forecasting projection models (Smart, Last Week, Last Year, Customized, Imported) and forecast metrics.
- [Entree Forecasting](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/entree-forecasting.md): Forecast by entree count using entree multipliers on Menu Item Categories and configure EPLH and Variable Labor Matrix settings.
- [Forecast Rules](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/forecast-rules.md): Forecast rules use a trigger and action to change default projection behavior for specific dates and are grouped by priority.
- [Create and Edit a Forecast Rule](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/create-and-edit-a-forecast-rule.md): Create or edit a forecast rule with a trigger (day, date, month, event) and action that overrides the default projection.
- [Closed Business Days](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/closed-business-days.md): Closed business days suppress forecast generation for sales and labor. Recalculation occurs when closure dates change.
- [Create a One-Off or Partial Closed Business Day Event](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/create-a-full-or-partial-closed-business-day-event.md): Create events for fully or partially closed business days to adjust sales forecasts for reduced or no revenue on specific dates.
- [Smart Labor](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/smart-labor.md): Smart Labor add-on enables the Labor Matrix, Hourly Forecasting, and additional Operations and Labor Dashboard widgets.

- [Archive or Restore POS Items](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/archive-or-restore-pos-items.md): Archive a POS item to set it inactive and restore it to active status to keep the POS item list clean and current.
- [Menu Item Separation](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/menu-item-separation.md): Menu item separation lets multi-concept organizations control which menu items from each concept display per location.

- [Edit Payroll Calendar](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/edit-payroll-calendar.md): Edit future pay dates on a published payroll calendar using List View. Past periods and frequency settings are locked from editing.
- [Delete Payroll Calendar](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/delete-payroll-calendar.md): Delete a payroll calendar after all periods are completed and no payments are pending. Deleting also removes the next year's calendar.
- [Payroll Calendar Wizard](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/payroll-calendar-wizard.md): Payroll Calendar Wizard — 3-step guide for pay company, frequency, start date, pay period review, and optional copy to other companies.

- [Payroll Hub Page](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/legacy-payroll-overview.md): Payroll Overview page — central hub for on-cycle and off-cycle pay runs with status, gross pay, employee counts, and summary cards.

- [Pay Run Details Screen](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/pay-run-details-page.md): Pay Run Details screen — all employee payments with hours, tips, gross/net pay, and error icons. Add employees and import earnings here.

- [Pay Run Approval Wizard](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/pay-run-approval-wizard.md): Pay Run Approval Wizard — shows gross pay, taxes, deductions, PTO, pay date, and total to fund before final irreversible approval.
- [Pull Labor Data](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/pull-labor-data.md): Pull labor data from POS imports and applied labor rules into the pay period. Requires DSS labor review before pulling.
- [Funding Page & Funding Details Report](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/funding-page-funding-details-report-1.md): Funding page in R365 Payroll — lists funding transactions for approved pay runs with amounts, dates, statuses, and payroll companies.
- [Returned Payments](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/returned-payments-1.md): View failed direct deposit distributions on the Returned Payments page, including employee, amount, and batch type.

- [Delete an Off-Cycle Pay Run](https://docs.restaurant365.com/docs/delete-an-off-cycle-pay-run.md): Delete an off-cycle pay run created in error from the Payroll Overview page using the menu button. Does not affect other pay runs.
